- **schedule-flow**: Schedule a stream to start and/or end at future timestamps, executed by Superfluid's keeper network. Automatically grants the FlowScheduler the flow-operator permissions it needs (create for --start, delete for --end) plus a SuperToken allowance when --start-amount is set. At least one of --start / --end is required; execution is permissionless but not guaranteed if the grants are revoked.
